# Skills · H · part 27

875 entries, hiivmind-corpus-enrich-headless to hire. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **hiivmind-corpus-enrich-headless**: Headless (non-interactive) enrichment of stale index entries. Re-scans changed source files to regenerate entry metadata (title, summary, tags, keywords, category), assigns concepts from the existing graph.yaml, verifies summaries against content, clears stale flags, re-renders, and re-embeds. Run after hiivmind-corpus-refresh-headless in automated pipelines, or standalone to repair a corpus with accumulated stale entries. Requires a v2 index (index.yaml). ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-enrich-headless))
- **hiivmind-corpus-graph**: View, validate, and edit a corpus concept graph (graph.yaml). Triggers: "graph", "show graph", "validate graph", "concept graph", "view concepts", "graph.yaml", "show relationships", "add concept", "add relationship". ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-graph))
- **hiivmind-corpus-init**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "create a corpus", "initialize documentation", "set up docs for a library", "index this project's docs", "create documentation corpus", or mentions wanting to create a new documentation corpus. Also triggers on "new corpus", "corpus for [library name]", or "hiivmind-corpus init". ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-init))
- **hiivmind-corpus-migrate**: Headless v1 → v2 index migration. Parses entry lines from index.md and index-*.md, cross-references .source/ for metadata, emits index.yaml plus a render: block, renders deterministically, and diff-checks ID parity against the original. Writes migrate-result.yaml (headless contract). Triggers: "migrate corpus", "migrate to v2", "convert index.md to index.yaml". ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-migrate))
- **hiivmind-corpus-navigate**: Navigate and query documentation from registered corpora. Use when users ask documentation questions, want to look up API references, or mention keywords matching registered corpora. Triggers: documentation, docs, lookup, search corpus, what does, how to, API reference. Auto-triggers when query contains keywords from any registered corpus (flyio, polars, etc.). ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-navigate))
- **hiivmind-corpus-refresh**: This skill should be used when the user asks to "refresh corpus", "sync documentation", "update corpus index", "check for upstream changes", "corpus is stale", "docs are outdated", or mentions that documentation sources have changed. - **hiivmind-corpus-refresh-headless**: Headless (non-interactive) variant of hiivmind-corpus-refresh for use by automated pipelines and scheduled tasks. Always runs in update mode, selects all stale sources automatically, and emits a machine-readable YAML result block instead of prose. Use hiivmind-corpus-refresh for interactive use. ([hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus](https://github.com/hiivmind/hiivmind-corpus/tree/HEAD/skills/hiivmind-corpus-refresh-headless))

Pick how each iteration's reflection is focused with --focus all (every failing val task), cyclic (one task at a time), or hardest-first (lowest-scoring first). ([nick-haffner/haffner-tech_ai-workflow-training](https://github.com/nick-haffner/haffner-tech_ai-workflow-training/tree/HEAD/plugins/ai-workflow-training/skills/hint))
- **hint**: Give a tiered hint for the current build-your-own-X step without revealing the implementation, the source coordinates, or the answer. Use whenever the learner is stuck on their bmox project — "I'm stuck", "give me a hint", "how do I even start this", "/bmox:hint" — INSTEAD of explaining the solution directly. ([ovltn/bmox](https://github.com/ovltn/bmox/tree/HEAD/skills/hint))
- **hint**: Dá a dica mais pequena que desbloqueia o estudante no ponto em que está no exercício atual ([sebastiaoteixeira/workshop-claude](https://github.com/sebastiaoteixeira/workshop-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/hint))
- **hint**: In an lstack learning vault (a folder containing lstack.yaml), give one escalating hint on the problem the user is stuck on without revealing the answer or any problem-specific step. Use for "/hint", "I'm stuck", "give me a hint", "nudge me on question 3". Skip when the folder has no lstack.yaml, when the user asks for the answer (/give-up), or when they are stuck on code, a build, or a tool rather than a study problem. ([sscw32/lstack](https://github.com/sscw32/lstack/tree/HEAD/skills/hint))
- **hint**: Get a targeted hint when you're stuck on the current chapter. Fetches the spec from GitHub, reads your code, gives a nudge without giving away the answer. ([tbhbooks/tbh-skill](https://github.com/tbhbooks/tbh-skill/tree/HEAD/skills/hint))
- **hint**: Provide a progressive hint for the current exercise without giving the answer. Uses Socratic questioning to guide understanding. Use when user says "hint", "help", "I'm stuck", or is struggling with an exercise. ([tcole333/python-learning-module](https://github.com/tcole333/python-learning-module/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/hint))
- **hint**: Help the user get unstuck with a graduated hint ladder instead of the answer. Use when they say they are stuck, ask for a hint, or run it. ([vinayaktyagi10/claude](https://github.com/vinayaktyagi10/claude/tree/HEAD/plugins/coach/skills/hint))
- **hint**: LeetCode 문제를 풀다 막혔을 때 단계적으로 힌트를 주는 스킬. "힌트 줘", "막혔어", "모르겠어", "어떻게 접근하지" 같은 요청이 오면 이 스킬을 실행한다. ([yoouyeon/claude-plugin](https://github.com/yoouyeon/claude-plugin/tree/HEAD/plugins/leetlog/skills/hint))

"hint 태그 발행", "힌트 내보내기", "이 셀 발행하자", "이 레시피 배포하자", "카탈로그 갱신", "비슷한 hint 찾아줘" 같은 지시에 발동. 수신자(배포받은 코드에이전트)의 토큰노믹스가 목적이며, 저작은 메인과 single 서브(승인된 자기 셀 · 별도 hint 클론)·카탈로그는 메인 단독으로 원격 발행 태그에서 파생한다. - **hintedbt-eval**: Evaluates cross-script machine translation quality for low-resource Indian languages (Hindi, Gujarati, Tamil) translating to English. It specifically probes how well models leverage back-translation augmented with quality and transliteration hints to handle noisy data and script conversion challenges. Use when the user wants to benchmark on IIT Bombay en-hi Corpus, WMT-2019 gu-en, TED2020, GNOME & Ubuntu, OPUS, WMT-2020 ta-en, GNOME, OPUS, WMT-2014 hi→en test, WMT-2019 gu→en test, WMT-2020 ta→en test, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports SacreBLEU. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/hintedbt-eval) · ★3)
- **hintel-alignbench-eval**: Evaluates multimodal large language models on vision-language tasks in Hindi and Telugu, measuring performance regression when transitioning from English to these Indian languages. It probes native-language visual question answering, mathematical reasoning, and multiple-choice comprehension across STEM and cultural domains. Use when the user wants to benchmark on HinTel-AlignBench, or asks about evaluating this task. Reports accuracy. ([qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool](https://github.com/qhjqhj00/research-skills-pool/tree/HEAD/skill-factory/output/hintel-alignbench-eval) · ★3)

- **hintme**: Use this skill when the user is stuck in a video game and wants help without spoilers. Triggers: 'hintme', 'I'm stuck', 'what do I do next', 'hint me', 'game help', 'stuck in game', 'where do I go', 'how do I beat this', 'game screenshot help'. Analyzes game screenshots and gives progressive hints — nudge first, then clearer direction, then explicit answer. Works with any game. Do NOT use for: game recommendations, game reviews, non-gaming questions, or cheat codes. ([KeWang0622/hintme](https://github.com/KeWang0622/hintme/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **hinto-cli**: Use this skill whenever the user wants to work with Hinto AI from the terminal.
